// === ./src/main/java/egovframework/product/web/ProductController.java ===
package egovframework.product.web;

import egovframework.member.vo.MemberVO;
import egovframework.product.service.ProductService;
import egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO;
import egovframework.product.vo.SalesVO;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

@Controller
public class ProductController {

    @Autowired
    private ProductService productService;

    // 카테고리 접두어 매핑 (DB 조회 없이 Controller에서 직접 처리)
    private static final Map<String, String> PREFIX_MAP = new HashMap<String, String>() {{
        put("식품",   "FOOD");
        put("전자",   "ELEC");
        put("소모품", "CONS");
        put("원자재", "RAW");
        put("기타",   "ETC");
    }};

    private boolean isNotLoggedIn(HttpSession session) {
        return session.getAttribute("loginUser") == null;
    }

    // ── 상품 관리 ──────────────────────────────────
    @GetMapping("/product/list.do")
    public String productList(@ModelAttribute ProductVO vo, Model model, HttpSession session) {
        if (isNotLoggedIn(session)) return "redirect:/login.do";
        model.addAttribute("productList", productService.getProductList(vo));
        model.addAttribute("totalCount",  productService.getProductCount(vo));
        model.addAttribute("searchVO", vo);
        return "product/productList";
    }

    @PostMapping("/product/insert.do")
    public String productInsert(@ModelAttribute ProductVO vo, HttpSession session) {
        if (isNotLoggedIn(session)) return "redirect:/login.do";
        vo.setIsActive(1);
        productService.addProduct(vo);
        return "redirect:/product/list.do";
    }

    @PostMapping("/product/update.do")
    public String productUpdate(@ModelAttribute ProductVO vo, HttpSession session) {
        if (isNotLoggedIn(session)) return "redirect:/login.do";
        productService.modifyProduct(vo);
        return "redirect:/product/list.do";
    }

    @PostMapping("/product/delete.do")
    public String productDelete(@RequestParam Long productId, HttpSession session) {
        if (isNotLoggedIn(session)) return "redirect:/login.do";
        productService.removeProduct(productId);
        return "redirect:/product/list.do";
    }

    @GetMapping("/product/detail.do")
    @ResponseBody
    public String productDetail(@RequestParam Long productId, HttpSession session) {
        if (isNotLoggedIn(session)) return "UNAUTHORIZED";
        ProductVO p = productService.getProduct(productId);
        if (p == null) return "NOT_FOUND";
        return "{"
            + "\"productId\":"     + p.getProductId()   + ","
            + "\"productCode\":\"" + esc(p.getProductCode()) + "\","
            + "\"productName\":\"" + esc(p.getProductName()) + "\","
            + "\"category\":\""   + esc(p.getCategory())     + "\","
            + "\"unit\":\""       + esc(p.getUnit())          + "\","
            + "\"unitCost\":"     + p.getUnitCost()           + ","
            + "\"reorderPoint\":" + p.getReorderPoint()       + ","
            + "\"reorderQty\":"   + p.getReorderQty()         + ","
            + "\"isActive\":"     + p.getIsActive()
            + "}";
    }

    /**
     * 카테고리 선택 시 다음 상품코드 자동 채번
     * - DB 조회 없이 현재 목록에서 JS가 계산하도록 prefix만 반환
     * - 예외 발생 지점을 없애기 위해 완전히 단순화
     */
    @GetMapping("/product/nextCode.do")
    public void nextProductCode(@RequestParam(defaultValue = "") String category,
                                HttpSession session,
                                HttpServletResponse response) {
        response.setContentType("text/plain;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = null;
        try {
            out = response.getWriter();
            if (isNotLoggedIn(session)) {
                out.print("UNAUTHORIZED");
                return;
            }
            String prefix = PREFIX_MAP.containsKey(category)
                    ? PREFIX_MAP.get(category) : "ETC";

            // DB에서 마지막 번호 조회 시도, 실패하면 001 반환
            String nextCode;
            try {
                nextCode = productService.generateNextProductCode(category);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                nextCode = prefix + "-001";
            }
            out.print(nextCode);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            response.setStatus(500);
        } finally {
            if (out != null) out.flush();
        }
    }

    // ── 판매 현황 ──────────────────────────────────
    @GetMapping("/sales/list.do")
    public String salesList(@ModelAttribute SalesVO vo, Model model, HttpSession session) {
        if (isNotLoggedIn(session)) return "redirect:/login.do";
        model.addAttribute("salesList",  productService.getSalesList(vo));
        model.addAttribute("totalCount", productService.getSalesCount(vo));
        model.addAttribute("searchVO", vo);
        return "product/salesList";
    }

    @PostMapping("/sales/insert.do")
    public String salesInsert(@ModelAttribute SalesVO vo, HttpSession session) {
        if (isNotLoggedIn(session)) return "redirect:/login.do";
        MemberVO loginUser = (MemberVO) session.getAttribute("loginUser");
        vo.setCreatedBy(loginUser.getMemberId());
        if (vo.getStatus() == null || vo.getStatus().isEmpty()) vo.setStatus("DRAFT");
        productService.addSales(vo);
        return "redirect:/sales/list.do";
    }

    // ── 재고 현황 ──────────────────────────────────
    @GetMapping("/stock/list.do")
    public String stockList(@ModelAttribute ProductVO vo,
                            @RequestParam(value = "includeDefect", required = false) String includeDefect,
                            Model model, HttpSession session) {
        if (isNotLoggedIn(session)) return "redirect:/login.do";
        vo.setIncludeDefect(includeDefect);
        int lowStockCount  = productService.getLowStockCount();
        int normalOnlyCount = productService.getStockCount(vo); // 불량 제외 전체 (검색 조건 반영)
        if ("Y".equals(includeDefect)) {
            model.addAttribute("stockList",  productService.getStockListAll(vo));
            model.addAttribute("totalCount", productService.getStockCountAll(vo));
        } else {
            model.addAttribute("stockList",  productService.getStockList(vo));
            model.addAttribute("totalCount", normalOnlyCount);
        }
        model.addAttribute("lowStockCount",  lowStockCount);
        model.addAttribute("normalCount",    normalOnlyCount - lowStockCount); // 불량 제외 정상 재고
        model.addAttribute("searchVO", vo);
        return "product/stockList";
    }

    @PostMapping("/stock/update.do")
    public String stockUpdate(@ModelAttribute ProductVO vo, HttpSession session) {
        if (isNotLoggedIn(session)) return "redirect:/login.do";
        productService.modifyStock(vo);
        return "redirect:/stock/list.do";
    }

    private String esc(String s) {
        return s == null ? "" : s.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("\"", "\\\"");
    }
}

package egovframework.salary.mapper;

import egovframework.salary.vo.SalaryVO;
import org.egovframe.rte.psl.dataaccess.mapper.Mapper;
import java.util.List;

@Mapper
public interface SalaryMapper {
    List<SalaryVO> selectSalaryList();
    SalaryVO       selectSalaryByMemberId(String memberId);
    void           insertSalary(SalaryVO salary);
    void           updateSalary(SalaryVO salary);
}

// === ./src/main/java/egovframework/salary/service/impl/SalaryServiceImpl.java ===
package egovframework.salary.service.impl;

import egovframework.salary.mapper.SalaryMapper;
import egovframework.salary.service.SalaryService;
import egovframework.salary.vo.SalaryVO;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import java.util.List;

@Service
public class SalaryServiceImpl implements SalaryService {

    @Autowired
    private SalaryMapper salaryMapper;

    @Override
    public List<SalaryVO> getSalaryList() {
        return salaryMapper.selectSalaryList();
    }

    @Override
    public SalaryVO getSalaryByMemberId(String memberId) {
        return salaryMapper.selectSalaryByMemberId(memberId);
    }

    @Override
    public void saveSalary(SalaryVO salary) {
        salaryMapper.insertSalary(salary);
    }
}

// === ./src/main/java/egovframework/salary/web/SalaryController.java ===
package egovframework.salary.web;

import egovframework.member.vo.MemberVO;
import egovframework.salary.service.SalaryService;
import egovframework.salary.vo.SalaryVO;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Controller;
import org.springframework.ui.Model;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.util.List;

@Controller
@RequestMapping("/salary")
public class SalaryController {

    @Autowired
    private SalaryService salaryService;

    /** 급여 목록 (ADMIN/운영팀만) */
    @GetMapping("/list.do")
    public String salaryList(Model model, HttpSession session) {
        if (session.getAttribute("loginUser") == null) return "redirect:/login.do";
        MemberVO loginUser = (MemberVO) session.getAttribute("loginUser");
        // ADMIN 또는 운영팀만 접근 가능
        if (!loginUser.getRole().equals("ADMIN") && !("운영팀".equals(loginUser.getDepartment()))) {
            return "redirect:/main.do";
        }
        List<SalaryVO> salaryList = salaryService.getSalaryList();
        model.addAttribute("salaryList", salaryList);
        return "salary/salaryList";
    }

    /** 내 급여 조회 (본인만) */
    @GetMapping("/my.do")
    public String mySalary(Model model, HttpSession session) {
        if (session.getAttribute("loginUser") == null) return "redirect:/login.do";
        MemberVO loginUser = (MemberVO) session.getAttribute("loginUser");
        SalaryVO salary = salaryService.getSalaryByMemberId(loginUser.getMemberId());
        model.addAttribute("salary", salary);
        return "salary/mySalary";
    }
}

// === ./src/main/resources/egovframework/spring/context-datasource.xml ===
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<beans xmlns="http://www.springframework.org/schema/beans"
    xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
    xmlns:context="http://www.springframework.org/schema/context"
    xsi:schemaLocation="
        http://www.springframework.org/schema/beans
        http://www.springframework.org/schema/beans/spring-beans-4.0.xsd
        http://www.springframework.org/schema/context
        http://www.springframework.org/schema/context/spring-context-4.0.xsd">
	
	<!-- 테스트 실행용 
	<jdbc:embedded-database id="dataSource" type="HSQL">
		<jdbc:script location= "classpath:/db/sampledb.sql"/>
	</jdbc:embedded-database>
	--> 
     
     
    <!-- hsql (테스트용 메모리 DB)
    <bean id="dataSource" class="org.apache.commons.dbcp2.BasicDataSource" destroy-method="close">
        <property name="driverClassName" value="net.sf.log4jdbc.DriverSpy"/>
        <property name="url" value="jdbc:log4jdbc:hsqldb:hsql://localhost/sampledb"/>
        <property name="username" value="sa"/>
    </bean>
    -->  
    
    <!-- Mysql (POM에서 commons-dbcp, mysql-connector-java 관련 라이브러리 설정 ) 
    -->
    <context:property-placeholder location="classpath:/db.properties"/>
    
    <bean id="dataSource" class="org.apache.commons.dbcp2.BasicDataSource" destroy-method="close">
        <property name="driverClassName" value="com.mysql.cj.jdbc.Driver"/>
        <property name="url"      value="${db.url}"/>
        <property name="username" value="${db.username}"/>
        <property name="password" value="${db.password}"/>
    </bean>
	<!-- flyway (DB 연결 설정) 
	<bean id="flywayConfig" class="org.flywaydb.core.api.configuration.ClassicConfiguration">
	    <property name="dataSource" ref="dataSource" />
	    <property name="baselineOnMigrate" value="true" />
	    <property name="baselineVersion" value="0" />
	    <property name="locations" value="classpath:db/migration" />
	</bean>

	<bean id="flyway" class="org.flywaydb.core.Flyway" init-method="migrate">
    	<constructor-arg ref="flywayConfig" />
	</bean>
	-->
    
    <!-- oracle (POM에서 commons-dbcp, ojdbc(라이센스 사항으로 별도로 배포되지 않음) 관련 라이브러리 설정)
    <bean id="dataSource" class="org.apache.commons.dbcp2.BasicDataSource" destroy-method="close">
        <property name="driverClassName" value="oracle.jdbc.driver.OracleDriver"/>
        <property name="url" value="jdbc:oracle:thin:@127.0.0.1:1521:example" />
        <property name="username" value="user"/>
        <property name="password" value="password"/>
    </bean>
    -->
    
</beans>

            value="egovframework.member.mapper,
                   egovframework.product.mapper,
                   egovframework.approval.mapper,
                   egovframework.order.mapper,
                   egovframework.salary.mapper,
                   egovframework.dept.mapper,
                   egovframework.supplier.mapper,
                   egovframework.customer.mapper" />
    </bean>
</beans>

// === ./src/main/resources/egovframework/spring/context-transaction.xml ===
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<beans xmlns="http://www.springframework.org/schema/beans" xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
	xmlns:tx="http://www.springframework.org/schema/tx"
	xmlns:aop="http://www.springframework.org/schema/aop"
	xsi:schemaLocation="http://www.springframework.org/schema/beans http://www.springframework.org/schema/beans/spring-beans-4.0.xsd
    	http://www.springframework.org/schema/tx http://www.springframework.org/schema/tx/spring-tx-4.0.xsd
    	http://www.springframework.org/schema/aop http://www.springframework.org/schema/aop/spring-aop-4.0.xsd">

	<bean id="txManager" class="org.springframework.jdbc.datasource.DataSourceTransactionManager">
		<property name="dataSource" ref="dataSource"/>
	</bean>

	<tx:advice id="txAdvice" transaction-manager="txManager">
		<tx:attributes>
			<tx:method name="*" rollback-for="Exception"/>
		</tx:attributes>
	</tx:advice>

	<aop:config>
		<aop:pointcut id="requiredTx" expression="execution(* egovframework.example.sample..impl.*Impl.*(..))"/>
		<aop:advisor advice-ref="txAdvice" pointcut-ref="requiredTx" />
	</aop:config>

</beans>

// === ./src/main/resources/egovframework/sqlmap/approval/Approvalmapper.xml ===
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="egovframework.approval.mapper.ApprovalMapper">

    <resultMap id="docResultMap" type="egovframework.approval.vo.ApprovalDocVO">
        <id     property="docId"          column="doc_id"/>
        <result property="docNo"          column="doc_no"/>
        <result property="docType"        column="doc_type"/>
        <result property="title"          column="title"/>
        <result property="status"         column="status"/>
        <result property="rejectReason"   column="reject_reason"/>
        <result property="customerId"     column="customer_id"/>
        <result property="supplierId"     column="supplier_id"/>
        <result property="requesterId"    column="requester_id"/>
        <result property="approverId"     column="approver_id"/>
        <result property="requestedAt"    column="requested_at"/>
        <result property="approvedAt"     column="approved_at"/>
        <result property="createdAt"      column="created_at"/>
        <result property="partnerName"    column="partner_name"/>
        <result property="partnerAddress" column="partner_address"/>
        <result property="requesterName"  column="requester_name"/>
        <result property="approverName"   column="approver_name"/>
    </resultMap>

    <resultMap id="itemResultMap" type="egovframework.approval.vo.ApprovalDocItemVO">
        <id     property="itemId"      column="item_id"/>
        <result property="docId"       column="doc_id"/>
        <result property="productId"   column="product_id"/>
        <result property="qty"         column="qty"/>
        <result property="unitCost"    column="unit_cost"/>
        <result property="remarks"     column="remarks"/>
        <result property="productCode" column="product_code"/>
        <result property="productName" column="product_name"/>
        <result property="unit"        column="unit"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- ── 목록 조회 ─────────────────────────── -->
    <select id="selectApprovalList"
            parameterType="egovframework.approval.vo.ApprovalDocVO"
            resultMap="docResultMap">
        SELECT d.doc_id, d.doc_no, d.doc_type, d.title, d.status,
               d.reject_reason, d.customer_id, d.supplier_id,
               d.requester_id, d.approver_id,
               DATE_FORMAT(d.requested_at, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%i') AS requested_at,
               DATE_FORMAT(d.approved_at,  '%Y-%m-%d %H:%i') AS approved_at,
               DATE_FORMAT(d.created_at,   '%Y-%m-%d %H:%i') AS created_at,
               COALESCE(c.customer_name, s.supplier_name) AS partner_name,
               COALESCE(c.address,       s.address)       AS partner_address,
               r.name AS requester_name,
               a.name AS approver_name
        FROM   approval_doc d
        LEFT JOIN customer c ON d.customer_id = c.customer_id
        LEFT JOIN supplier s ON d.supplier_id = s.supplier_id
        LEFT JOIN member   r ON d.requester_id = r.member_id
        LEFT JOIN member   a ON d.approver_id  = a.member_id
        <where>
            <if test="searchStatus != null and searchStatus != ''">
                d.status = #{searchStatus}
            </if>
            <if test="searchDocType != null and searchDocType != ''">
                AND d.doc_type = #{searchDocType}
            </if>
            <if test="searchTitle != null and searchTitle != ''">
                AND d.title LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchTitle}, '%')
            </if>
        </where>
        ORDER BY d.created_at DESC
    </select>

    <!-- ── 건수 ──────────────────────────────── -->
    <select id="selectApprovalCount"
            parameterType="egovframework.approval.vo.ApprovalDocVO"
            resultType="int">
        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM approval_doc d
        <where>
            <if test="searchStatus != null and searchStatus != ''">
                d.status = #{searchStatus}
            </if>
            <if test="searchDocType != null and searchDocType != ''">
                AND d.doc_type = #{searchDocType}
            </if>
            <if test="searchTitle != null and searchTitle != ''">
                AND d.title LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchTitle}, '%')
            </if>
        </where>
    </select>

    <!-- ── 탭별 건수 (전체 카운트용) ─────────── -->
    <select id="selectStatusCounts" resultType="java.util.Map">
        SELECT
            COUNT(*) AS total,
            SUM(CASE WHEN status='PENDING'     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS pending,
            SUM(CASE WHEN status='IN_PROGRESS' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS inProgress,
            SUM(CASE WHEN status='REJECTED'    THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS rejected,
            SUM(CASE WHEN status='APPROVED'    THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS approved
        FROM approval_doc
    </select>

    <!-- ── 상세 조회 ──────────────────────────── -->
    <select id="selectApproval" parameterType="long" resultMap="docResultMap">
        SELECT d.doc_id, d.doc_no, d.doc_type, d.title, d.status, d.reject_reason,
               d.customer_id, d.supplier_id, d.requester_id, d.approver_id,
               DATE_FORMAT(d.requested_at, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%i') AS requested_at,
               DATE_FORMAT(d.approved_at,  '%Y-%m-%d %H:%i') AS approved_at,
               DATE_FORMAT(d.created_at,   '%Y-%m-%d %H:%i') AS created_at,
               COALESCE(c.customer_name, s.supplier_name) AS partner_name,
               COALESCE(c.address,       s.address)       AS partner_address,
               r.name AS requester_name,
               a.name AS approver_name
        FROM   approval_doc d
        LEFT JOIN customer c ON d.customer_id = c.customer_id
        LEFT JOIN supplier s ON d.supplier_id = s.supplier_id
        LEFT JOIN member   r ON d.requester_id = r.member_id
        LEFT JOIN member   a ON d.approver_id  = a.member_id
        WHERE  d.doc_id = #{docId}
    </select>

    <!-- ── 상품 라인 조회 ────────────────────── -->
    <select id="selectApprovalItems" parameterType="long" resultMap="itemResultMap">
        SELECT i.item_id, i.doc_id, i.product_id,
               i.qty, i.unit_cost, i.remarks,
               p.product_code, p.product_name, p.unit
        FROM   approval_doc_item i
        JOIN   product p ON i.product_id = p.product_id
        WHERE  i.doc_id = #{docId}
        ORDER BY i.item_id
    </select>

    <!-- ── 등록 ──────────────────────────────── -->
    <insert id="insertApprovalDoc"
            parameterType="egovframework.approval.vo.ApprovalDocVO"
            useGeneratedKeys="true" keyProperty="docId">
        INSERT INTO approval_doc
            (doc_no, doc_type, title, status,
             customer_id, supplier_id, requester_id, approver_id,
             requested_at, created_at)
        VALUES
            (#{docNo}, #{docType}, #{title}, #{status},
             #{customerId}, #{supplierId}, #{requesterId}, #{approverId},
             NOW(), NOW())
    </insert>

    <insert id="insertApprovalItem"
            parameterType="egovframework.approval.vo.ApprovalDocItemVO">
        INSERT INTO approval_doc_item
            (doc_id, product_id, qty, unit_cost, remarks)
        VALUES
            (#{docId}, #{productId}, #{qty}, #{unitCost}, #{remarks})
    </insert>

    <!-- ── 상태 변경 ──────────────────────────── -->
    <update id="updateApprovalStatus"
            parameterType="egovframework.approval.vo.ApprovalDocVO">
        UPDATE approval_doc
        SET    status = #{status}
               <if test="rejectReason != null">, reject_reason = #{rejectReason}</if>
               <if test="approverId   != null">, approver_id   = #{approverId}</if>
               <if test="status == 'APPROVED' or status == 'REJECTED'">
                   , approved_at = NOW()
               </if>
        WHERE  doc_id = #{docId}
    </update>

    <!-- ── 삭제 ──────────────────────────────── -->
    <delete id="deleteApprovalItems" parameterType="long">
        DELETE FROM approval_doc_item WHERE doc_id = #{docId}
    </delete>

    <delete id="deleteApprovalDoc" parameterType="long">
        DELETE FROM approval_doc WHERE doc_id = #{docId} AND status = 'DRAFT'
    </delete>

</mapper>

// === ./src/main/resources/egovframework/sqlmap/customer/CustomerMapper.xml ===

        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

        <result property="customerName" column="customer_name"/>
        <result property="contact"      column="contact"/>
        <result property="phone"        column="phone"/>
        <result property="address"      column="address"/>
        <result property="isActive"     column="is_active"/>
    </resultMap>

        FROM   customer

    </select>

// === ./src/main/resources/egovframework/sqlmap/dept/DeptMapper.xml ===
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
    "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="egovframework.dept.mapper.DeptMapper">

    <resultMap id="deptResultMap" type="egovframework.dept.vo.DeptVO">
        <id     property="deptId"      column="dept_id"/>
        <result property="deptName"    column="dept_name"/>
        <result property="managerId"   column="manager_id"/>
        <result property="managerName" column="manager_name"/>
        <result property="empCount"    column="emp_count"/>
        <result property="createdAt"   column="created_at"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 부서 목록 조회 (소속 직원 수 포함) -->
    <select id="selectDeptList" resultMap="deptResultMap">
        SELECT d.dept_id, d.dept_name, d.manager_id,
               m.name AS manager_name,
               COUNT(e.member_id) AS emp_count,
               d.created_at
        FROM   department d
        LEFT JOIN member m ON d.manager_id = m.member_id
        LEFT JOIN member e ON e.dept_id = d.dept_id AND e.status = 'ACTIVE'
        GROUP BY d.dept_id, d.dept_name, d.manager_id, m.name, d.created_at
        ORDER BY d.dept_id
    </select>

    <!-- 부서 단건 조회 -->
    <select id="selectDeptById" parameterType="String" resultMap="deptResultMap">
        SELECT dept_id, dept_name, manager_id, created_at
        FROM   department
        WHERE  dept_id = #{deptId}
    </select>

    <!-- 부서 추가 -->
    <insert id="insertDept" parameterType="egovframework.dept.vo.DeptVO">
        INSERT INTO department (dept_id, dept_name, manager_id)
        VALUES (#{deptId}, #{deptName}, #{managerId, jdbcType=VARCHAR})
    </insert>

    <!-- 부서 수정 -->
    <update id="updateDept" parameterType="egovframework.dept.vo.DeptVO">
        UPDATE department
        SET dept_name  = #{deptName},
            manager_id = #{managerId, jdbcType=VARCHAR}
        WHERE dept_id = #{deptId}
    </update>

    <!-- 부서 삭제 -->
    <delete id="deleteDept" parameterType="String">
        DELETE FROM department
        WHERE dept_id = #{deptId}
    </delete>
    
    <!--  부서코드 중복 체크 -->
    <select id="countDuplicate" resultType="int">
    SELECT COUNT(*) FROM department
    WHERE dept_name = #{deptName}
    <if test="deptId != null and deptId != ''">
        AND dept_id != #{deptId}
    </if>
</select>

<select id="countByDeptId" resultType="int">
    SELECT COUNT(*) FROM department WHERE dept_id = #{deptId}
</select>

<select id="countByDeptName" resultType="int">
    SELECT COUNT(*) FROM department
    WHERE dept_name = #{deptName}
    <if test="excludeId != null and excludeId != ''">
        AND dept_id != #{excludeId}
    </if>
</select>

</mapper>

// === ./src/main/resources/egovframework/sqlmap/MemberMapper.xml ===
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
    "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="egovframework.member.mapper.MemberMapper">

    <resultMap id="memberResultMap" type="egovframework.member.vo.MemberVO">
        <id     property="memberId"   column="member_id"/>
        <result property="password"   column="password"/>
        <result property="name"       column="name"/>
        <result property="email"      column="email"/>
        <result property="role"       column="role"/>
        <result property="status"     column="status"/>
        <result property="department" column="department"/>
        <result property="empNo"      column="emp_no"/>
        <result property="birthDate"  column="birth_date"/>
        <result property="phone"      column="phone"/>
        <result property="position"   column="position"/>
        <result property="gender"     column="gender"/>
        <result property="deptId"   column="dept_id"/>
		<result property="deptName" column="dept_name"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 회원가입 INSERT (status=PENDING으로 저장) -->
    <insert id="insertMember" parameterType="egovframework.member.vo.MemberVO">
        INSERT INTO member (
            member_id, password, name, email, role,
            status, department, birth_date, phone, gender
        ) VALUES (
            #{memberId}, #{password}, #{name}, #{email, jdbcType=VARCHAR}, 'USER',
            'PENDING', #{department, jdbcType=VARCHAR}, #{birthDate, jdbcType=VARCHAR},
            #{phone, jdbcType=VARCHAR}, #{gender, jdbcType=VARCHAR}
        )
    </insert>

  <!-- status 상관없이 아이디 중복체크용 조회 -->
   <select id="selectMemberById" parameterType="String" resultMap="memberResultMap">
    SELECT member_id, password, name, email, role, status
    FROM   member
    WHERE  member_id = #{memberId}
  </select>

    <!-- 로그인 시 status=ACTIVE 인 사람만 조회 -->
    <select id="selectMember" parameterType="String" resultMap="memberResultMap">
        SELECT member_id, password, name, email, role, status,
               department, emp_no, birth_date, phone, position, gender
        FROM   member
        WHERE  member_id = #{memberId}
        AND    status = 'ACTIVE'
    </select>

    <!-- 결재자 목록 (전체 회원) -->
    <select id="selectMemberList" resultMap="memberResultMap">
        SELECT member_id, name, role, status, department
        FROM   member
        ORDER BY name
    </select>

    <!-- 가입 신청 목록 (PENDING 상태) -->
    <select id="selectPendingList" resultMap="memberResultMap">
        SELECT member_id, name, email, department, birth_date, phone, gender, created_at
        FROM   member
        WHERE  status = 'PENDING'
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 승인/거절 처리 -->
    <update id="updateMemberStatus" parameterType="egovframework.member.vo.MemberVO">
        UPDATE member
        SET status = #{status}
        WHERE member_id = #{memberId}
    </update>
<!-- 직원 목록 조회 (ACTIVE 상태만, 이름/사번 검색 가능) -->
  <select id="selectEmpList" parameterType="egovframework.member.vo.MemberVO" resultMap="memberResultMap">
    SELECT m.member_id, m.name, m.email, m.role, m.status,
           m.department, m.emp_no, m.phone, m.position, m.gender, m.birth_date,
           m.dept_id, d.dept_name
    FROM   member m
    LEFT JOIN department d ON m.dept_id = d.dept_id
    WHERE  m.status = 'ACTIVE'
    <if test="keyword != null and keyword != ''">
        AND (m.name LIKE CONCAT('%', #{keyword}, '%')
        OR m.emp_no LIKE CONCAT('%', #{keyword}, '%'))
    </if>
    <if test="deptId != null and deptId != ''">
        AND m.dept_id = #{deptId}
    </if>
    ORDER BY m.name
</select>



    <!-- 직원 정보 수정 -->
    <update id="updateMember" parameterType="egovframework.member.vo.MemberVO">
    UPDATE member
    SET name       = #{name},
        email      = #{email, jdbcType=VARCHAR},
        department = #{department, jdbcType=VARCHAR},
        position   = #{position, jdbcType=VARCHAR},
        phone      = #{phone, jdbcType=VARCHAR},
        role       = #{role},
        emp_no     = #{empNo, jdbcType=VARCHAR}
    WHERE member_id = #{memberId}
</update>

    <!-- 직원 삭제 -->
    <delete id="deleteMember" parameterType="String">
        DELETE FROM member
        WHERE member_id = #{memberId}
    </delete>
    
</mapper>

// === ./src/main/resources/egovframework/sqlmap/order/Orderreceiptmapper.xml ===
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="egovframework.order.mapper.OrderReceiptMapper">

    <!-- ── ResultMap ─────────────────────────── -->
    <resultMap id="receiptResultMap" type="egovframework.order.vo.OrderReceiptVO">
        <id     property="receiptId"    column="receipt_id"/>
        <result property="receiptNo"    column="receipt_no"/>
        <result property="docId"        column="doc_id"/>
        <result property="docType"      column="doc_type"/>
        <result property="orderDate"    column="order_date"/>
        <result property="partnerName"  column="partner_name"/>
        <result property="requesterId"  column="requester_id"/>
        <result property="approverId"   column="approver_id"/>
        <result property="totalAmount"  column="total_amount"/>
        <result property="createdAt"    column="created_at"/>
        <result property="requesterName" column="requester_name"/>
        <result property="approverName"  column="approver_name"/>
        <result property="docNo"        column="doc_no"/>
    </resultMap>

    <resultMap id="itemResultMap" type="egovframework.order.vo.OrderReceiptItemVO">
        <id     property="itemId"      column="item_id"/>
        <result property="receiptId"   column="receipt_id"/>
        <result property="productId"   column="product_id"/>
        <result property="productCode" column="product_code"/>
        <result property="productName" column="product_name"/>
        <result property="unit"        column="unit"/>
        <result property="qty"         column="qty"/>
        <result property="unitCost"    column="unit_cost"/>
        <result property="amount"      column="amount"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- ── 목록 조회 ─────────────────────────── -->
    <select id="selectReceiptList"
            parameterType="egovframework.order.vo.OrderReceiptVO"
            resultMap="receiptResultMap">
        SELECT r.receipt_id, r.receipt_no, r.doc_id, r.doc_type,
               DATE_FORMAT(r.order_date, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%i') AS order_date,
               r.partner_name, r.requester_id, r.approver_id,
               r.total_amount,
               DATE_FORMAT(r.created_at, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%i') AS created_at,
               req.name AS requester_name,
               apr.name AS approver_name,
               d.doc_no
        FROM   order_receipt r
        LEFT JOIN member       req ON r.requester_id = req.member_id
        LEFT JOIN member       apr ON r.approver_id  = apr.member_id
        LEFT JOIN approval_doc d   ON r.doc_id       = d.doc_id
        <where>
            <if test="searchDocType != null and searchDocType != ''">
                r.doc_type = #{searchDocType}
            </if>
            <if test="searchKeyword != null and searchKeyword != ''">
                AND (r.receipt_no    LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%')
                  OR r.partner_name  LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%'))
            </if>
        </where>
        ORDER BY r.created_at DESC
    </select>

    <!-- ── 건수 ──────────────────────────────── -->
    <select id="selectReceiptCount"
            parameterType="egovframework.order.vo.OrderReceiptVO"
            resultType="int">
        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM order_receipt r
        <where>
            <if test="searchDocType != null and searchDocType != ''">
                r.doc_type = #{searchDocType}
            </if>
            <if test="searchKeyword != null and searchKeyword != ''">
                AND (r.receipt_no   LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%')
                  OR r.partner_name LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%'))
            </if>
        </where>
    </select>

    <!-- ── 상세 조회 ──────────────────────────── -->
    <select id="selectReceipt" parameterType="long" resultMap="receiptResultMap">
        SELECT r.receipt_id, r.receipt_no, r.doc_id, r.doc_type,
               DATE_FORMAT(r.order_date, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%i') AS order_date,
               r.partner_name, r.requester_id, r.approver_id,
               r.total_amount,
               DATE_FORMAT(r.created_at, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%i') AS created_at,
               req.name AS requester_name,
               apr.name AS approver_name,
               d.doc_no
        FROM   order_receipt r
        LEFT JOIN member       req ON r.requester_id = req.member_id
        LEFT JOIN member       apr ON r.approver_id  = apr.member_id
        LEFT JOIN approval_doc d   ON r.doc_id       = d.doc_id
        WHERE  r.receipt_id = #{receiptId}
    </select>

    <!-- ── 상품 라인 조회 ────────────────────── -->
    <select id="selectReceiptItems" parameterType="long" resultMap="itemResultMap">
        SELECT item_id, receipt_id, product_id,
               product_code, product_name, unit,
               qty, unit_cost, amount
        FROM   order_receipt_item
        WHERE  receipt_id = #{receiptId}
        ORDER BY item_id
    </select>

    <!-- ── 주문 등록 ──────────────────────────── -->
    <insert id="insertReceipt"
            parameterType="egovframework.order.vo.OrderReceiptVO"
            useGeneratedKeys="true" keyProperty="receiptId">
        INSERT INTO order_receipt
            (receipt_no, doc_id, doc_type, order_date,
             partner_name, requester_id, approver_id, total_amount, created_at)
        VALUES
            (#{receiptNo}, #{docId}, #{docType}, NOW(),
             #{partnerName}, #{requesterId}, #{approverId}, #{totalAmount}, NOW())
    </insert>

    <!-- ── 상품 라인 등록 ────────────────────── -->
    <insert id="insertReceiptItem"
            parameterType="egovframework.order.vo.OrderReceiptItemVO">
        INSERT INTO order_receipt_item
            (receipt_id, product_id, product_code, product_name, unit, qty, unit_cost, amount)
        VALUES
            (#{receiptId}, #{productId}, #{productCode}, #{productName},
             #{unit}, #{qty}, #{unitCost}, #{amount})
    </insert>

    <!-- ── doc_id로 중복 체크 ───────────────── -->
    <select id="countByDocId" parameterType="long" resultType="int">
        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM order_receipt WHERE doc_id = #{docId}
    </select>

</mapper>
// === ./src/main/resources/egovframework/sqlmap/product/ProductMapper.xml ===
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"

<mapper namespace="egovframework.product.mapper.ProductMapper">

    <!-- ResultMap -->
    <resultMap id="productResultMap" type="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO">
        <id     property="productId"      column="product_id"/>
        <result property="productCode"    column="product_code"/>
        <result property="productName"    column="product_name"/>
        <result property="category"       column="category"/>
        <result property="unit"           column="unit"/>
        <result property="reorderPoint"   column="reorder_point"/>
        <result property="reorderQty"     column="reorder_qty"/>
        <result property="unitCost"       column="unit_cost"/>
        <result property="isActive"       column="is_active"/>
        <result property="createdAt"      column="created_at"/>
        <result property="qtyOnHand"      column="qty_on_hand"/>
        <result property="qtyReserved"    column="qty_reserved"/>
        <result property="warehouseName"  column="warehouse_name"/>
        <result property="warehouseId"    column="warehouse_id"/>
        <result property="warehouseType"  column="warehouse_type"/>
    </resultMap>

    <resultMap id="salesResultMap" type="egovframework.product.vo.SalesVO">
        <id     property="soId"           column="so_id"/>
        <result property="soNo"           column="so_no"/>
        <result property="customerId"     column="customer_id"/>
        <result property="customerName"   column="customer_name"/>
        <result property="orderDate"      column="order_date"/>
        <result property="deliveryDate"   column="delivery_date"/>
        <result property="status"         column="status"/>
        <result property="createdBy"      column="created_by"/>
        <result property="createdAt"      column="created_at"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!--  상품 관리                                                      -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <select id="selectProductList" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO"
            resultMap="productResultMap">
        SELECT product_id, product_code, product_name, category, unit,
               reorder_point, reorder_qty, unit_cost, is_active, created_at
        FROM   PRODUCT
        <where>
            <if test="searchKeyword != null and searchKeyword != ''">
                AND (product_name LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%')
                  OR product_code LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%'))
            </if>
            <if test="searchCategory != null and searchCategory != ''">
                AND category = #{searchCategory}
            </if>
        </where>
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
    </select>

    <select id="selectProductCount" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO"
            resultType="int">
        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM PRODUCT
        <where>
            <if test="searchKeyword != null and searchKeyword != ''">
                AND (product_name LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%')
                  OR product_code LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%'))
            </if>
            <if test="searchCategory != null and searchCategory != ''">
                AND category = #{searchCategory}
            </if>
        </where>
    </select>

    <select id="selectProduct" parameterType="Long" resultMap="productResultMap">
        SELECT product_id, product_code, product_name, category, unit,
               reorder_point, reorder_qty, unit_cost, is_active, created_at
        FROM   PRODUCT
        WHERE  product_id = #{productId}
    </select>

    <insert id="insertProduct" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO"
            useGeneratedKeys="true" keyProperty="productId">
        INSERT INTO PRODUCT (product_code, product_name, category, unit,
                             reorder_point, reorder_qty, unit_cost, is_active)
        VALUES (#{productCode}, #{productName}, #{category}, #{unit},
                #{reorderPoint}, #{reorderQty}, #{unitCost}, #{isActive})
    </insert>

    <update id="updateProduct" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO">
        UPDATE PRODUCT
        SET product_code  = #{productCode},
            product_name  = #{productName},
            category      = #{category},
            unit          = #{unit},
            reorder_point = #{reorderPoint},
            reorder_qty   = #{reorderQty},
            unit_cost     = #{unitCost},
            is_active     = #{isActive}
        WHERE product_id = #{productId}
    </update>

    <update id="deleteProduct" parameterType="long">
    	UPDATE product SET is_active = 0 WHERE product_id = #{productId}
	</update>

    <!-- 상품 삭제 시 INVENTORY 먼저 제거 (FK 제약 방지) -->
    <delete id="deleteInventoryByProductId" parameterType="Long">
        DELETE FROM INVENTORY WHERE product_id = #{productId}
    </delete>

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!--  판매 현황 (SALES_ORDER)                                        -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <select id="selectSalesList" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.SalesVO"
            resultMap="salesResultMap">
        SELECT s.so_id, s.so_no, s.customer_id, c.customer_name,
               DATE_FORMAT(s.order_date, '%Y-%m-%d')    AS order_date,
               DATE_FORMAT(s.delivery_date, '%Y-%m-%d') AS delivery_date,
               s.status, s.created_by,
               DATE_FORMAT(s.created_at, '%Y-%m-%d')    AS created_at
        FROM   SALES_ORDER s
        LEFT JOIN CUSTOMER c ON s.customer_id = c.customer_id
        <where>
            <if test="searchKeyword != null and searchKeyword != ''">
                AND (c.customer_name LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%')
                  OR s.so_no         LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%'))
            </if>
            <if test="searchStatus != null and searchStatus != ''">
                AND s.status = #{searchStatus}
            </if>
            <if test="dateFrom != null and dateFrom != ''">
                AND s.order_date &gt;= #{dateFrom}
            </if>
            <if test="dateTo != null and dateTo != ''">
                AND s.order_date &lt;= #{dateTo}
            </if>
        </where>
        ORDER BY s.created_at DESC
    </select>

    <select id="selectSalesCount" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.SalesVO"
            resultType="int">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM   SALES_ORDER s
        LEFT JOIN CUSTOMER c ON s.customer_id = c.customer_id
        <where>
            <if test="searchKeyword != null and searchKeyword != ''">
                AND (c.customer_name LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%')
                  OR s.so_no         LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%'))
            </if>
            <if test="searchStatus != null and searchStatus != ''">
                AND s.status = #{searchStatus}
            </if>
            <if test="dateFrom != null and dateFrom != ''">
                AND s.order_date &gt;= #{dateFrom}
            </if>
            <if test="dateTo != null and dateTo != ''">
                AND s.order_date &lt;= #{dateTo}
            </if>
        </where>
    </select>

    <insert id="insertSales" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.SalesVO"
            useGeneratedKeys="true" keyProperty="soId">
        INSERT INTO SALES_ORDER (so_no, customer_id, order_date, delivery_date, status, created_by)
        VALUES (#{soNo}, #{customerId}, #{orderDate}, #{deliveryDate}, #{status}, #{createdBy})
    </insert>

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!--  재고 현황 - 불량창고 제외 (기본)                                  -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <select id="selectStockList" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO"
            resultMap="productResultMap">
        SELECT p.product_id, p.product_code, p.product_name, p.category, p.unit,
               p.reorder_point, p.unit_cost, p.is_active,
               IFNULL(i.qty_on_hand, 0)   AS qty_on_hand,
               IFNULL(i.qty_reserved, 0)  AS qty_reserved,
               w.warehouse_name, w.warehouse_id,
               w.type                     AS warehouse_type
        FROM   PRODUCT p
        LEFT JOIN INVENTORY i  ON p.product_id  = i.product_id
        LEFT JOIN WAREHOUSE  w ON i.warehouse_id = w.warehouse_id
        WHERE  (w.type IS NULL OR w.type != 'DEFECT')
        <if test="searchKeyword != null and searchKeyword != ''">
            AND (p.product_name LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%')
              OR p.product_code LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%'))
        </if>
        <if test="searchCategory != null and searchCategory != ''">
            AND p.category = #{searchCategory}
        </if>
        ORDER BY
            CASE WHEN IFNULL(i.qty_on_hand, 0) &lt;= p.reorder_point THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
            i.qty_on_hand ASC
    </select>

    <select id="selectStockCount" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO"
            resultType="int">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM   PRODUCT p
        LEFT JOIN INVENTORY i ON p.product_id  = i.product_id
        LEFT JOIN WAREHOUSE  w ON i.warehouse_id = w.warehouse_id
        WHERE  (w.type IS NULL OR w.type != 'DEFECT')
        <if test="searchKeyword != null and searchKeyword != ''">
            AND (p.product_name LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%')
              OR p.product_code LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%'))
        </if>
        <if test="searchCategory != null and searchCategory != ''">
            AND p.category = #{searchCategory}
        </if>
    </select>

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!--  재고 현황 - 불량창고 포함 전체                                    -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->
<select id="selectStockListAll" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO"
        resultMap="productResultMap">
    SELECT p.product_id, p.product_code, p.product_name, p.category, p.unit,
           p.reorder_point, p.unit_cost, p.is_active,
           IFNULL(i.qty_on_hand, 0)   AS qty_on_hand,
           IFNULL(i.qty_reserved, 0)  AS qty_reserved,
           w.warehouse_name, w.warehouse_id,
           w.type                     AS warehouse_type
    FROM   PRODUCT p
    LEFT JOIN INVENTORY i  ON p.product_id  = i.product_id
    LEFT JOIN WAREHOUSE  w ON i.warehouse_id = w.warehouse_id
    <where>
        <if test="searchKeyword != null and searchKeyword != ''">
            AND (p.product_name LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%')
              OR p.product_code LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%'))
        </if>
        <if test="searchCategory != null and searchCategory != ''">
            AND p.category = #{searchCategory}
        </if>
    </where>
    ORDER BY
        CASE WHEN w.type = 'DEFECT' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
        CASE WHEN IFNULL(i.qty_on_hand, 0) &lt;= p.reorder_point THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
        i.qty_on_hand ASC
</select>

    <select id="selectStockCountAll" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO"
            resultType="int">
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM   PRODUCT p
        LEFT JOIN INVENTORY i ON p.product_id  = i.product_id
        LEFT JOIN WAREHOUSE  w ON i.warehouse_id = w.warehouse_id
        <where>
            <if test="searchKeyword != null and searchKeyword != ''">
                AND (p.product_name LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%')
                  OR p.product_code LIKE CONCAT('%', #{searchKeyword}, '%'))
            </if>
            <if test="searchCategory != null and searchCategory != ''">
                AND p.category = #{searchCategory}
            </if>
        </where>
    </select>



    <!-- MAIN 창고 ID 조회 (재고 반영 시 사용) -->
    <select id="selectMainWarehouseId" resultType="long">
        SELECT warehouse_id FROM warehouse WHERE type = 'MAIN' AND is_active = 1 LIMIT 1
    </select>

    <!-- 특정 상품의 MAIN 창고 inventory 존재 여부 -->
    <select id="selectInventoryByProductAndWarehouse" resultType="long"
            parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO">
        SELECT IFNULL(SUM(qty_on_hand), -1)
        FROM   inventory
        WHERE  product_id   = #{productId}
          AND  warehouse_id = #{warehouseId}
    </select>

    <!-- 재고 수량 변동 — UPSERT (레코드 없으면 INSERT, 있으면 UPDATE) -->
    <insert id="updateInventoryQty" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO">
        INSERT INTO inventory (product_id, warehouse_id, qty_on_hand, qty_reserved, last_updated)
        VALUES (#{productId}, #{warehouseId}, #{qtyDelta}, 0, NOW())
        ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
            qty_on_hand  = qty_on_hand + #{qtyDelta},
            last_updated = NOW()
    </insert>

    <!-- 재고 조정 (STOCK_ADJ): 절대값으로 SET -->
    <insert id="setInventoryQty" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO">
        INSERT INTO inventory (product_id, warehouse_id, qty_on_hand, qty_reserved, last_updated)
        VALUES (#{productId}, #{warehouseId}, #{qtyDelta}, 0, NOW())
        ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
            qty_on_hand  = #{qtyDelta},
            last_updated = NOW()
    </insert>

    <!-- 재고 이동 이력 INSERT -->
    <insert id="insertStockMovement" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO">
        INSERT INTO stock_movement
            (product_id, warehouse_id, movement_type, qty,
             unit_cost, ref_order_id, ref_order_type, remarks, created_by, created_at)
        VALUES
            (#{productId}, #{warehouseId}, #{movementType}, #{qtyDelta},
             #{unitCost}, #{refOrderId}, #{refOrderType}, #{remarks}, #{createdBy}, NOW())
    </insert>

    <update id="updateStock" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO">
        UPDATE INVENTORY
        SET qty_on_hand = #{qtyOnHand}
        WHERE product_id   = #{productId}
          AND warehouse_id = #{warehouseId}
    </update>

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!--  대시보드                                                        -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <select id="selectTotalProductCount" resultType="int">
        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM PRODUCT WHERE is_active = 1
    </select>

    <select id="selectLowStockCount" resultType="int">
    	SELECT COUNT(*)
    	FROM   PRODUCT p
    	JOIN   INVENTORY i ON p.product_id  = i.product_id
    	JOIN   WAREHOUSE w ON i.warehouse_id = w.warehouse_id
    	WHERE  i.qty_on_hand &lt;= p.reorder_point
      	AND  p.is_active = 1
      	AND  w.type != 'DEFECT'
	</select>

    <!-- 재고 부족 상품 목록 (대시보드용, 최대 5건) -->
    <select id="selectLowStockList" resultMap="productResultMap">
        SELECT p.product_id, p.product_code, p.product_name, p.unit,
               p.reorder_point,
               IFNULL(SUM(i.qty_on_hand), 0) AS qty_on_hand,
               ROUND(IFNULL(SUM(i.qty_on_hand), 0) / NULLIF(p.reorder_point, 0) * 100) AS stock_pct
        FROM   product p
        JOIN   inventory i ON p.product_id  = i.product_id
        JOIN   warehouse w ON i.warehouse_id = w.warehouse_id
        WHERE  p.is_active = 1
        AND    w.type != 'DEFECT'
        GROUP BY p.product_id, p.product_code, p.product_name, p.unit, p.reorder_point
        HAVING IFNULL(SUM(i.qty_on_hand), 0) &lt;= p.reorder_point
        ORDER BY (IFNULL(SUM(i.qty_on_hand), 0) / NULLIF(p.reorder_point, 0)) ASC
        LIMIT 5
    </select>

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!--  자동 채번                                                       -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <select id="selectLastProductCodeByCategory" parameterType="String" resultType="String">
        SELECT product_code
        FROM   PRODUCT
        WHERE  category = #{category}
        ORDER BY product_id DESC
        LIMIT 1
    </select>

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!--  창고 / INVENTORY                                              -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!-- 기본 창고: DEFECT 아닌 것 중 첫 번째 -->
    <select id="selectDefaultWarehouseId" resultType="Long">
        SELECT warehouse_id
        FROM   WAREHOUSE
        WHERE  type != 'DEFECT'
        ORDER BY warehouse_id ASC
        LIMIT 1
    </select>

    <!-- 상품 등록 시 INVENTORY 레코드 자동 생성 -->
    <insert id="insertInventory" parameterType="egovframework.product.vo.ProductVO">
        INSERT INTO INVENTORY (product_id, warehouse_id, qty_on_hand, qty_reserved)
        VALUES (#{productId}, #{warehouseId}, #{qtyOnHand}, 0)
    </insert>

</mapper>
// === ./src/main/resources/egovframework/sqlmap/salary/SalaryMapper.xml ===
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
    "http://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="egovframework.salary.mapper.SalaryMapper">

    <resultMap id="salaryResultMap" type="egovframework.salary.vo.SalaryVO">
        <id     property="salaryId"    column="salary_id"/>
        <result property="memberId"    column="member_id"/>
        <result property="name"        column="name"/>
        <result property="department"  column="department"/>
        <result property="position"    column="position"/>
        <result property="baseSalary"  column="base_salary"/>
        <result property="payYear"     column="pay_year"/>
        <result property="payMonth"    column="pay_month"/>
        <result property="createdAt"   column="created_at"/>
    </resultMap>

    <!-- 전체 급여 목록 (직급별 기본급 JOIN) -->
    <select id="selectSalaryList" resultMap="salaryResultMap">
        SELECT s.salary_id, s.member_id, m.name, m.department, m.position,
               COALESCE(sg.base_salary, 0) AS base_salary,
               s.pay_year, s.pay_month, s.created_at
        FROM   salary s
        JOIN   member m ON s.member_id = m.member_id
        LEFT JOIN salary_grade sg ON m.position = sg.position
        ORDER BY s.pay_year DESC, s.pay_month DESC, m.name
    </select>

    <!-- 개인 급여 조회 -->
    <select id="selectSalaryByMemberId" parameterType="String" resultMap="salaryResultMap">
        SELECT s.salary_id, s.member_id, m.name, m.department, m.position,
               COALESCE(sg.base_salary, 0) AS base_salary,
               s.pay_year, s.pay_month, s.created_at
        FROM   salary s
        JOIN   member m ON s.member_id = m.member_id
        LEFT JOIN salary_grade sg ON m.position = sg.position
        WHERE  s.member_id = #{memberId}
        ORDER BY s.pay_year DESC, s.pay_month DESC
    </select>

    <!-- 급여 등록 -->
    <insert id="insertSalary" parameterType="egovframework.salary.vo.SalaryVO">
        INSERT INTO salary (member_id, base_salary, pay_year, pay_month)
        VALUES (#{memberId},
                (SELECT base_salary FROM salary_grade WHERE position =
                    (SELECT position FROM member WHERE member_id = #{memberId})),
                #{payYear}, #{payMonth})
    </insert>

    <!-- 급여 수정 -->
    <update id="updateSalary" parameterType="egovframework.salary.vo.SalaryVO">
        UPDATE salary
        SET base_salary = #{baseSalary}
        WHERE salary_id = #{salaryId}
    </update>

</mapper>

        <result property="supplierName" column="supplier_name"/>
        <result property="contact"      column="contact"/>
        <result property="phone"        column="phone"/>
        <result property="address"      column="address"/>
        <result property="isActive"     column="is_active"/>
    </resultMap>

    <select id="selectSupplierList" resultMap="supplierResultMap">
        SELECT supplier_id, supplier_name, contact, phone, address, is_active
        FROM   supplier
        WHERE  is_active = 1
        ORDER BY supplier_name
    </select>

<!--

   This file contains the default Struts Validator pluggable validator
   definitions.  It should be placed somewhere under /WEB-INF and
   referenced in the struts-config.xml under the plug-in element
   for the ValidatorPlugIn.

      <plug-in className="org.apache.struts.validator.ValidatorPlugIn">
        <set-property property="pathnames" value="/WEB-INF/validator-rules.xml,
                                                  /WEB-INF/validation.xml"/>
      </plug-in>

   These are the default error messages associated with
   each validator defined in this file.  They should be
   added to your projects ApplicationResources.properties
   file or you can associate new ones by modifying the
   pluggable validators msg attributes in this file.

   # Struts Validator Error Messages
   errors.required={0} is required.
   errors.minlength={0} can not be less than {1} characters.
   errors.maxlength={0} can not be greater than {1} characters.
   errors.invalid={0} is invalid.

   errors.byte={0} must be a byte.
   errors.short={0} must be a short.
   errors.integer={0} must be an integer.
   errors.long={0} must be a long.
   errors.float={0} must be a float.
   errors.double={0} must be a double.

   errors.date={0} is not a date.
   errors.range={0} is not in the range {1} through {2}.
   errors.creditcard={0} is an invalid credit card number.
   errors.email={0} is an invalid e-mail address.

<form-validation>

   <global>

      <validator name="required"
            classname="org.springmodules.validation.commons.FieldChecks"
               method="validateRequired"
         methodParams="java.lang.Object,
                       org.apache.commons.validator.ValidatorAction,
                       org.apache.commons.validator.Field,
                       org.springframework.validation.Errors"
                  msg="errors.required">

         <javascript><![CDATA[
            function validateRequired(form) {
                var isValid = true;
                var focusField = null;
                var i = 0;
                var fields = new Array();
                oRequired = new required();                                
                for (x in oRequired) {
                var field = form[oRequired[x][0]];                 

           if ((field.type == 'hidden' ||
               field.type == 'text' ||
               field.type == 'textarea' ||
               field.type == 'file' ||
               field.type == 'radio' ||
               field.type == 'checkbox' ||
               field.type == 'select-one' ||
               field.type == 'password')) {
               var value = '';
               // get field's value
               if (field.type == "select-one") {
                   var si = field.selectedIndex;
                   if (si >= 0) {
                       value = field.options[si].value;
                   }
               } else if (field.type == 'radio' || field.type == 'checkbox') {
                   if (field.checked) {
                       value = field.value;
                   }
               } else {
                   value = field.value;
               }
               if (trim(value).length == 0) {
                   if ((i == 0) && (field.type != 'hidden')) {
                       focusField = field;
                   }
                   fields[i++] = oRequired[x][1];
                   isValid = false;
               }
           } else if (field.type == "select-multiple") { 
               var numOptions = field.options.length;
               lastSelected=-1;
               for(loop=numOptions-1;loop>=0;loop--) {
                   if(field.options[loop].selected) {
                       lastSelected = loop;
                       value = field.options[loop].value;
                       break;
                   }
               }
               if(lastSelected < 0 || trim(value).length == 0) {
                   if(i == 0) {
                       focusField = field;
                   }
                   fields[i++] = oRequired[x][1];
                   isValid=false;
               }
           } else if ((field.length > 0) && (field[0].type == 'radio' || field[0].type == 'checkbox')) {
               isChecked=-1;
               for (loop=0;loop < field.length;loop++) {
                   if (field[loop].checked) {
                       isChecked=loop;
                       break; // only one needs to be checked
                   }
               }
               if (isChecked < 0) {
                   if (i == 0) {
                       focusField = field[0];
                   }
                   fields[i++] = oRequired[x][1];
                   isValid=false;
               }
           }
                }
                if (fields.length > 0) {
                   focusField.focus();
                   alert(fields.join('\n'));
                }
                return isValid;
            }
            
            // Trim whitespace from left and right sides of s.
            function trim(s) {
                return s.replace( /^\s*/, "" ).replace( /\s*$/, "" );
            }
            
            ]]>
         </javascript>

      </validator>

            function validateMinLength(form) {
                var isValid = true;
                var focusField = null;
                var i = 0;
                var fields = new Array();
                oMinLength = new minlength();
                for (x in oMinLength) {
                    var field = form[oMinLength[x][0]];
                    
                    if (field.type == 'text' ||
                        field.type == 'textarea') {
                        
                        var iMin = parseInt(oMinLength[x][2]("minlength"));
                        if ((trim(field.value).length > 0) && (field.value.length < iMin)) {
                            if (i == 0) {
                                focusField = field;
                            }
                            fields[i++] = oMinLength[x][1];
                            isValid = false;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (fields.length > 0) {
                   focusField.focus();
                   alert(fields.join('\n'));
                }
                return isValid;
            }]]>

      <validator name="mask"
            classname="org.springmodules.validation.commons.FieldChecks"
               method="validateMask"
         methodParams="java.lang.Object,
                       org.apache.commons.validator.ValidatorAction,
                       org.apache.commons.validator.Field,
                       org.springframework.validation.Errors"
                       
              depends=""
                  msg="errors.invalid">

         <javascript><![CDATA[
            function validateMask(form) {
                var isValid = true;
                var focusField = null;
                var i = 0;
                var fields = new Array();
                oMasked = new mask();
                for (x in oMasked) {
                    var field = form[oMasked[x][0]];
                    
                    if ((field.type == 'text' || 
                         field.type == 'textarea') && 
                         (field.value.length > 0)) {
                        
                        if (!matchPattern(field.value, oMasked[x][2]("mask"))) {
                            if (i == 0) {
                                focusField = field;
                            }
                            fields[i++] = oMasked[x][1];
                            isValid = false;
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                if (fields.length > 0) {
                   focusField.focus();
                   alert(fields.join('\n'));
                }
                return isValid;
            }

            function matchPattern(value, mask) {
               return mask.exec(value);
            }]]>
         </javascript>

      </validator>

         <javascript><![CDATA[
            function validateByte(form) {
                var bValid = true;
                var focusField = null;
                var i = 0;
                var fields = new Array();
                oByte = new ByteValidations();
                for (x in oByte) {
                	var field = form[oByte[x][0]];
                	
                    if (field.type == 'text' ||
                        field.type == 'textarea' ||
                        field.type == 'select-one' ||
						field.type == 'radio') {

						var value = '';
						// get field's value
						if (field.type == "select-one") {
							var si = field.selectedIndex;
							if (si >= 0) {
								value = field.options[si].value;
							}
						} else {
							value = field.value;
						}
                        
                        if (value.length > 0) {
                            if (!isAllDigits(value)) {
                                bValid = false;
                                if (i == 0) {
                                    focusField = field;
                                }
                                fields[i++] = oByte[x][1];

                            } else {

	                            var iValue = parseInt(value);
	                            if (isNaN(iValue) || !(iValue >= -128 && iValue <= 127)) {
	                                if (i == 0) {
	                                    focusField = field;
	                                }
	                                fields[i++] = oByte[x][1];
	                                bValid = false;
	                            }
                            }
						}
						
                    }
                }
                if (fields.length > 0) {
                   focusField.focus();
                   alert(fields.join('\n'));
                }
                return bValid;
            }]]>
         </javascript>

      <validator name="integer"

         <javascript><![CDATA[
            function validateInteger(form) {
                var bValid = true;
                var focusField = null;
                var i = 0;
                var fields = new Array();
                oInteger = new IntegerValidations();
                for (x in oInteger) {
                	var field = form[oInteger[x][0]];

                    if (field.type == 'text' ||
                        field.type == 'textarea' ||
                        field.type == 'select-one' ||
                        field.type == 'radio') {
                        
                        var value = '';
						// get field's value
						if (field.type == "select-one") {
							var si = field.selectedIndex;
						    if (si >= 0) {
							    value = field.options[si].value;
						    }
						} else {
							value = field.value;
						}
                        
                        if (value.length > 0) {
                        
                            if (!isAllDigits(value)) {
                                bValid = false;
                                if (i == 0) {
	                                focusField = field;
	                            }
						        fields[i++] = oInteger[x][1];
						        
                            } else {
	                            var iValue = parseInt(value);
	                            if (isNaN(iValue) || !(iValue >= -2147483648 && iValue <= 2147483647)) {
	                                if (i == 0) {
	                                    focusField = field;
	                                }
	                                fields[i++] = oInteger[x][1];
	                                bValid = false;
	                           }
                           }
                       }
                    }
                }
                if (fields.length > 0) {
                   focusField.focus();
                   alert(fields.join('\n'));
                }
                return bValid;
            }

            function isAllDigits(argvalue) {
                argvalue = argvalue.toString();
                var validChars = "0123456789";
                var startFrom = 0;
                if (argvalue.substring(0, 2) == "0x") {
                   validChars = "0123456789abcdefABCDEF";
                   startFrom = 2;
                } else if (argvalue.charAt(0) == "0") {
                   validChars = "01234567";
                   startFrom = 1;
                } else if (argvalue.charAt(0) == "-") {
                    startFrom = 1;
                }
                
                for (var n = startFrom; n < argvalue.length; n++) {
                    if (validChars.indexOf(argvalue.substring(n, n+1)) == -1) return false;
                }
                return true;
            }]]>
         </javascript>

      </validator>

      <validator name="float"
            classname="org.springmodules.validation.commons.FieldChecks"
               method="validateFloat"
         methodParams="java.lang.Object,
                       org.apache.commons.validator.ValidatorAction,
                       org.apache.commons.validator.Field,
                       org.springframework.validation.Errors"
                       
              depends=""
                  msg="errors.float"
       jsFunctionName="FloatValidations">

         <javascript><![CDATA[
            function validateFloat(form) {
                var bValid = true;
                var focusField = null;
                var i = 0;
                var fields = new Array();
                oFloat = new FloatValidations();
                for (x in oFloat) {
                	var field = form[oFloat[x][0]];
                	
                    if (field.type == 'text' ||
                        field.type == 'textarea' ||
                        field.type == 'select-one' ||
                        field.type == 'radio') {
                        
                    	var value = '';
						// get field's value
						if (field.type == "select-one") {
							var si = field.selectedIndex;
							if (si >= 0) {
							    value = field.options[si].value;
							}
						} else {
							value = field.value;
						}
                        
                        if (value.length > 0) {
                            // remove '.' before checking digits
                            var tempArray = value.split('.');
                            var joinedString= tempArray.join('');

                            if (!isAllDigits(joinedString)) {
                                bValid = false;
                                if (i == 0) {
                                    focusField = field;
                                }
                                fields[i++] = oFloat[x][1];

                            } else {
	                            var iValue = parseFloat(value);
	                            if (isNaN(iValue)) {
	                                if (i == 0) {
	                                    focusField = field;
	                                }
	                                fields[i++] = oFloat[x][1];
	                                bValid = false;
	                            }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (fields.length > 0) {
                   focusField.focus();
                   alert(fields.join('\n'));
                }
                return bValid;
            }]]>
         </javascript>

      </validator>
